9181 — stick standing shoulder mobilization in external rotation

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 9181 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Using a stick to guide the arm, you rotate the shoulder outward. It improves external rotation range.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand holding a stick with both hands, elbows tucked at your sides and bent.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 9181.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Stand holding a stick with both hands, elbows tucked at your sides and bent.

Legs Feet

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand holding a stick with both hands, elbows tucked at your sides and bent. 9181.json#/instructions/0
  2. Push the stick with the stronger hand to rotate the other arm outward. 9181.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your elbow glued to your side. 9181.json#/instructions/2
  4. Move only as far as is comfortable. 9181.json#/instructions/3
  5. Return to the start slowly. 9181.json#/instructions/4
  6. Switch sides and repeat. 9181.json#/instructions/5

9246 — wide stance thoracic rotation

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 9246 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

In a wide stance you rotate your upper back to loosen the thoracic spine. It helps with trunk twisting.
